Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the bayesian space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 309 and the convergence rate is O(625/n^2).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to bayesian structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 309/625 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
